Pseudo for proxy_opt(n, p0, beta, iters, step):
- p = normalize(p0)
- y = log(p+1e-12)
- For each iter:
  - a=p; c=fft_convolve(a,a) (real length 2n-1)
  - L, grad_p = softmax_logsumexp_c(c, beta) & D.
  - grad_y = grad_p * p - p * dot(p, grad_p)
  - Adam update y with lr decaying.
  - p = softmax(y)
  - score exact = 2n*max(c) (we already have c maybe) update best.
Return best.

Let's design a custom optimizer:
- y = log(p)+small (sum y? not needed)
- m,v Adam states zeros.
- lr array? scalar maybe 0.05 initially, decay to 0.005.

- For each it:
  p=softmax(y) (maybe p sum exactly1; for large n, expshift)
  compute proxy and grad_y
  if grad norm NaN break
  Adam step:
    t+=1
    m = (1-b1)m+b1*gy
    v = (1-b2)v+b2*gy^2
    y -= lr * m_hat/(sqrt(v_hat)+eps)
    y -= y.mean()? To remove drift? Since p softmax invariant to adding constant, we can y -= y.mean() to prevent overflow. But gradient sum should zero; subtract mean after step.
  p=softmax(y)
  score = 2n*max(c) (using c already) ; update best if finite.
- To help escape local minima, periodically add random Gaussian noise to y (small) and accept if not too bad? Adam optimizer:
def refine_sequence(n, p0, max_steps, beta_start, beta_end, lr_start, lr_end, start_time, end_time, tag):
    p = normalize(p0)
    y = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    y -= y.mean()  # keep small
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    b1=0.9;b2=0.999;eps=1e-8
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score_fft(p)
    t=0
    # Could vary beta: beta = beta_start*(beta_end/beta_start)**(it/map)
    for it in range(max_steps):
       if time > end_time: break
       # if score plateau, maybe increase beta faster or add noise
       # compute
       p, c, cmax, proxy, gy = forward_gradient(p, beta)
       # But if p passed, avoid recompute c in forward? We'll implement forward returns all.
       # update best if score < best_score - 1e-12
       # Adam
       t+=1
       m=...
       v=...
       mhat=m/(1-b1**t); vhat=...
       step = lr * mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
       y -= step
       y -= y.mean()
       # optional noise: if it%500< or no improvement, y += rng.normal(0, noise_scale)
    return best,best_score

But forward_gradient takes p and returns c, gy; it does softmax? We maintain y. Compute p=softmax(y) each iteration (or after update). Could use p from previous update; ensure sum1 exactly.
Softmax: y_shift = y - y.max(); p = exp(y_shift); p/=sum. This ensures nonneg. Could roundoff.

Gradient computation in one function:
def gradient_for_p(p, beta, Lc, Lcorr, fft cache?):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(p,Lc)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,n=Lc)[:2n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0)
    cmax=np.max(c)
    scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
    # If beta*cmax large and c-cmax negative large, scaled underflows okay.
    z=np.exp(scaled - scaled.max()) # max 0
    zsum=z.sum()
    q=z/zsum
    # proxy = cmax + log(zsum)/beta
    # C = cross-correlation
    ar=p[::-1]
    fw=np.fft.rfft(q,Lcorr)
    fr=np.fft.rfft(ar,Lcorr)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fw*fr,n=Lcorr)
    C=conv[n-1:2*n-2]
    D=2*p*C
    # adjoint to y
    grad_p = D
    py = p*grad_p
    grad_y = py - p*np.dot(p, py)
    # Due to softmax invariance, sum grad_y maybe 0; clip nan
    score=2*n*cmax
    return p, cmax, score, proxy, grad_y

Let's craft code skeleton:
```python
"""
Optimizes nonnegative step heights for objective 2*n*max(conv(p,p))/sum(p)^2.
Uses FFT-based smooth log-sum-exp surrogate, Adam-like ascent in log-sum simplex,
diverse parametric/random initializers, low-resolution warm start, and block
local search. Returns sequence of same length as height_sequence_1 when available.
"""
import numpy as np, time, math, sys, random? (use np.random)
try: import scipy.optimize as sco (for maybe)
except: pass

Implement `next_power_two(x)`:
```
def next_power_two(x):
    p=1
    while p < x: p <<=1
    return p
```

`normalize_nonneg(arr, n=None)`:
```
arr=np.asarray(arr,dtype=float)
arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000.0) # maybe scale? If all zero return ones
s=arr.sum()
if s <= 0 or not finite: return np.ones(n or len(arr))/...
arr = arr/s
return arr
```
For initial, if p sum not1 normalize. If max >1000? p sum1 so max≤1 for n? no, but if not normalized, clip. For output p sum1, no issue.

`score_fft_array(p, return_c=False)`:
```
p = np.asarray(p,float); n=len
p=p.clip(0,1000); s=p.sum(); if s<1e-12 return inf; p=p/s
L=next_power_two(max(1,2*n-1))
fa=np.fft.rfft(p,n=L)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,n=L)[:2*n-1]
c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
# maybe renormalize c sum to 1? FFT error; if csum>0: c/=csum? But sum should1; scaling c sum to1 might bias if error. We can sum not.
cmax=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
score=2.0*n*cmax
if return_c: return score,c
```
Convolution of p sum1: sum c= psum^2=1. Due to FFT, sum may be 0.999999; okay.

`forward_gradient(p, beta, Lc, Lcorr, n)`:
```
fa=np.fft.rfft(p,n=Lc)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,n=Lc)[:2*n-1]
c=np.maximum(c,0)
cmax=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
if cmax <=0: return 0, cmax, 2n*0, zero grad
# stable softmax
scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
# To avoid all underflow if scaled large negative, max is 0 but if cmax not unique? scaled max 0. z=np.exp(scaled) ; if zsum inf? scaled <=0, exp <=1, zsize small; safe.
z=np.exp(scaled)
zsum=float(z.sum())
if zsum <=0 or not finite: z=np.zeros_like(c); idx=np.argmax(c); z[idx]=1; zsum=1
q=z/zsum
proxy = cmax + math.log(zsum)/beta
# C conv
ar=p[::-1]
fw=np.fft.rfft(q,n=Lcorr)
fr=np.fft.rfft(ar,n=Lcorr)
conv=np.fft.irfft(fw*fr,n=Lcorr)
C=conv[n-1:2*n-2]
# Due to numerical, C maybe small negative; no need? D derivative should nonnegative? p and q nonneg -> C nonneg; clip.
C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
D=2.0*p*C
py=p*D
gross=float(np.dot(p, py))
grad_y=py - p*gross
# enforce finite
if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_y)): grad_y=np.zeros_like(p)
score=2.0*n*cmax
return p, cmax, score, proxy, grad_y
```
Wait q length 2n-1 but Lcorr may be larger; rfft q pads zeros. Good.
But C_i =∑_k q_k p_{k-i}. For i near n-1, k=i+j up to2n-2. conv slice as before. Need ensure conv values beyond? okay.

In forward, we already have p; but in loop p computed from y. We can return new p? no.

`softmax_from_y(y)`:
```
z=y - np.max(y)
p=np.exp(z)
s=p.sum()
if s<=0 or not finite: p=np.ones_like(y)/len
else p/=s
return p
```

`adam_refine(...)`:
- Inputs n, p0, max_steps, beta_start, beta_end, lr_start, lr_end, rng, start_time, end_time, tag, verbose.
- y = np.log(p0 + 1e-300); y -= np.mean(y)
- m,v arrays.
- Lc,Lcorr.
- best_score = score_fft_array(p0); best_p=p0.copy()
- last_imp = it; imp_count=0
- for it in range(max_steps):
   if time > end_time: break
   beta = beta_start * ((beta_end/beta_start) ** (it / max(1, max_steps))) maybe log interpolation. If beta_start=0? set min1.
   p=softmax_from_y(y)
   p, cmax, score, proxy, gy = forward_gradient(...)
   if score < best_score - 1e-10:
      best_score=score; best_p=p.copy(); last_imp=it; imp_count=0
   else: imp_count +=1
   # Adam update
   t=it+1
   m=(1-b1)*m + b1*gy
   v=(1-b2)*v + b2*gy*gy
   mhat=m/(1-b1**t); vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
   lr = lr_start + (lr_end-lr_start)*(it/max_steps)
   y -= lr * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
   y -= y.mean()
   # if imp_count > 200: noise
   if imp_count > 200 and it % 50 ==0:
       # amplitude based on imp_count
       amp = 0.05 * min(3.0, 1.0 + imp_count/1000.0)
       y += rng.normal(0.0, amp, size=n)
       y -= y.mean()
       m *=0.5; v*=0.5
   # if imp_count > 5000: break to next start?
   if imp_count > 5000: break
- return best_p, best_score

But beta interpolation with max_steps: If max_steps small, end not reached; okay.

Potential issue: We compute beta based on max_steps; if break early by time, beta at break. Good.

`block_search(n, p, rng, max_attempts, deadline, tag)`:
- best_p, best_score = p, score_fft(p)
- For attempt in range(max_attempts):
   if time > deadline break
   candidate = best_p.copy()
   # random type
   m = candidate.size
   if rng.random()<0.6:
       width = int(rng.integers(max(1,m//200), max(2,m//8)))
       start=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,m-width)))
       cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.25))
   elif rng.random()<0.3:
       # two opposite blocks
       width = int(rng.integers(m//50,m//5))
       start=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,m-width)))
       cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.3))
       cand2 = m-1 - (start+width-1); cand2=max(0,m1 - (start+width-1)?) Use reverse.
       set cand[m-1-(start+width-1): m-start] maybe.
   else:
       # global smooth random: multiply by exp(noise lowpass)
       noise=rng.normal(0,0.1,m); smooth by cumulative? Use rng Fourier: f=rng.normal(0,1,m)+1j*rng.normal(0,1,m); f[1:]*=1/np.sqrt(np.arange(1,m)); smooth=irfft(f)[:m]; cand *= exp(smooth)
   cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000); s=cand.sum(); if s<=0: continue; cand/=s
   sc=score_fft_array(cand)
   if sc<best_score-1e-10: best=cand; best_score=sc; attempt counter...
   else occasionally random restart from best with larger noise: if rng.random()<0.01: candidate *= exp(rng.normal(0,0.5)); etc.
- Return.

This is pseudo-random but top-level.

Implement `make_power_base(n, s_r, s_l, eps=0.0, rng=None)`:
```
i=np.arange(n)
right=(n - i)/(n)  # 1 to1/n
left=(i + 1)/(n)
val = right**(-s_r) * left**(-s_l)
if eps>0: right+=eps; left+=eps; recompute
val=val + 1e-12
return normalize
```

Length strategy detailed:
```
def propose_candidate(...):
  deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0,budget_s-10)
  parent = get_parent()
  if parent valid: N=len(parent); fallback=parent
  else: N=3809; fallback=np.ones(N)
  # If N too small for search? use N.
  # low_n if N>1024: 1024 else N
  low_n = min(N, 1024) if N > 1024 else N
  rng = np.random.default_rng(seed if not None else 42)
  candidates=[]  # list of p arrays and scores for full length? We'll optimize low then full.
  # Phase1: low optimization
  low_inits = generate_initial_patterns(low_n, rng, parent_resampled if parent)
  best low patterns = optimize low top ~8 for slice time maybe 5% budget each? Actually low cheap; run each 800 steps.
  # Keep top 5 low patterns.
  # Resample to N, add also direct full initial patterns.
  full_candidates = resampled top low + generate_initial_patterns(N, rng, parent)
  score full all, keep top K=10.
  # Phase2: Adam refine full each with time slice.
  for p in full_top:
      if time > deadline-180: break
      p, score = adam_refine(N,p, steps=..., deadline=end_time_slice)
      add top.
  # Phase3: block search on top patterns for remaining.
  final candidates list top maybe 10.
  # Evaluate exact each with evaluate_sequence, choose min.

else: q=np.full(n,p[0])
q=np.clip(q,0,1000)
s=q.sum()
if s<=0: q=np.ones(n)/n
else q/=s
return q
```

i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)

Code for `make_fourier_pattern`:
```
def make_fourier_pattern(n, rng, amp_scale=0.5, kmodes=8):
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    val=np.ones(n)
    phase0=2.0*np.pi*i/n
    for k in range(1,kmodes+1):
       a=rng.normal(0, amp_scale/(k**0.5))
       b=rng.normal(0, amp_scale/(k**0.5))
       th=k*phase0 + rng.uniform(0,2π)
       val += a*np.cos(th)+b*np.sin(th)
    val=np.maximum(val,1e-3)
    return normalize_nonneg(val,n)
```
If val negative clip; okay.

`make_smooth_random_pattern`:
```
x=rng.normal(size=n)
f=np.fft.rfft(x)
freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
freq[0]=1.0
f /= (1.0 + 40.0*freq*freq)
y=np.fft.irfft(f,n)
std=y.std()
if std > 1e-12: y=1.0 + 1.0*(y-y.mean())/std
else: y=np.ones(n)
return normalize_nonneg(np.maximum(y,1e-3),n)
```

def safe_output_sequence(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    p=np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0, posinf=1000, neginf=0)
    p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
    mx=p.max()
    if mx > 1000: p*=1000/mx # though clipped
    s=p.sum()
    if s <=0: p=np.ones_like(p)/max(1,len(p))
    return [float(x) for x in p]
```
If p sum1, fine. Note scaling after clipping: if original max>1000, clipping changes relative; scaling won't undo; but irrelevant.

```python
"""
FFT-based surrogate-gradient search for nonnegative step sequences.

The returned sequence has the same length as height_sequence_1 when that global
is available.  The objective is scale invariant, so all searches are performed
on probability vectors p (sum p = 1).  A smooth log-sum-exp surrogate of
max(conv(p,p)) is used with FFT evaluations and FFT adjoints to do Adam-style
optimization in log-sum/softmax coordinates.  The search uses several
parametric and random warm starts, optional low-resolution warm starts, and a
final stochastic block/local-search polish.
"""

import time
import math

import numpy as np


def get_parent_sequence():
    """Return height_sequence_1 as a clean 1-D float array, or None."""
    g = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if g is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size <= 0:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(arr.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return None
    return arr


def normalize_nonneg(arr, n=None):
    """Clip to nonnegative finite values and normalize to sum 1."""
    if n is None:
        n = len(arr)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 1
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size != n:
        if arr.size == 0:
            arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            arr = arr[:n] if arr.size > n else np.pad(arr, (0, n - arr.size), mode="edge")
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e3, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1e3)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        arr = arr / s
    return arr


def resample_pattern(p, n):
    """Linearly resample a probability pattern to length n and renormalize."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if p.size <= 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if p.size == n:
        return normalize_nonneg(p, n)
    if p.size == 1:
        p = np.full(n, float(p[0]), dtype=np.float64)
        return normalize_nonneg(p, n)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return normalize_nonneg(q, n)


def next_power_two(x):
    """Smallest power of two >= x."""
    p = 1
    x = int(max(1, x))
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def score_fft_array(p, return_c=False):
    """Score 2*n*max(full_conv(p,p)) for p normalized to sum 1, using FFT."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return (float("inf"), None) if return_c else float("inf")
    p = normalize_nonneg(p, n)
    L = next_power_two(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(p, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=L)[:max(1, 2 * n - 1)]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax
    if return_c:
        return score, c
    return score


def forward_gradient(p, beta, Lc, Lcorr):
    """
    Given p (sum 1), compute objects for minimizing
    (1/beta) * log(sum_k exp(beta * conv_k)) on the softmax simplex.

    Returns (p, cmax, exact_score_fft, surrogate_value, grad_y).
    """
    n = p.size
    p = normalize_nonneg(p, n)

    fa = np.fft.rfft(p, n=Lc)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=Lc)[:max(1, 2 * n - 1)]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    if c.size == 0:
        zero = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return p, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, zero

    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 1e-300:
        zero = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return p, cmax, 2.0 * float(n) * cmax, cmax, zero

    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    # scaled is always <= 0. exp is stable, but guard against all zeros.
    z = np.exp(scaled)
    zsum = float(z.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(zsum) or zsum <= 1e-300:
        z = np.zeros_like(c)
        z[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        zsum = 1.0
    q = z / zsum
    surrogate = cmax + math.log(zsum) / beta

    # C_i = sum_k q_k p_{k-i}.  This is a linear convolution q * p[::-1]
    # sampled at indices n-1 .. 2n-2.
    ar = p[::-1]
    fw = np.fft.rfft(q, n=Lcorr)
    fr = np.fft.rfft(ar, n=Lcorr)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fw * fr, n=Lcorr)
    C = conv[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)

    # gradient wrt p of surrogate: D_i = sum_k q_k * 2 p_i p_{k-i}
    grad_p = 2.0 * p * C
    gp_p = p * grad_p
    weighted = float(np.dot(p, gp_p))
    grad_y = gp_p - p * weighted

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_y)):
        grad_y = np.zeros_like(grad_y)

    return p, cmax, 2.0 * float(n) * cmax, surrogate, grad_y


def softmax_from_y(y):
    """Stable softmax to a probability vector."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    z = y - float(np.max(y))
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones_like(y) / float(y.size)
    else:
        p = p / s
    return p


def adam_refine(n, p0, beta_start, beta_end, max_steps, lr_start, lr_end,
                rng, end_time, verbose=False):
    """Adam-style refinement on the softmax simplex using a log-sum-exp surrogate."""
    p0 = normalize_nonneg(p0, n)
    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_score = score_fft_array(p0)

    y = np.log(p0 + 1e-300)
    y -= float(np.mean(y))
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-10

    Lc = next_power_two(2 * n - 1)
    Lcorr = next_power_two(3 * n - 2)

    stagnation = 0
    last_train_imp = 0

    for it in range(max(1, int(max_steps))):
        if time.monotonic() > end_time:
            break

        if max_steps > 1:
            frac = float(it) / float(max_steps - 1)
            beta = float(beta_start) * (float(beta_end) / float(beta_start)) ** frac
        else:
            beta = float(beta_end)
        beta = max(1.0, min(beta, 1e6))

        p = softmax_from_y(y)
        try:
            p, cmax, score, _, grad_y = forward_gradient(p, beta, Lc, Lcorr)
        except Exception:
            break

        if math.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = float(score)
            best_p = p.copy()
            stagnation = 0
        else:
            stagnation += 1

        t = it + 1
        m = (1.0 - b1) * m + b1 * grad_y
        v = (1.0 - b2) * v + b2 * (grad_y * grad_y)

        m_hat = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
        v_hat = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t)

        lr = lr_start + (lr_end - lr_start) * (float(it) / float(max(max_steps, 1)))
        step = lr * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + eps)

        y = y - step
        y -= float(np.mean(y))

        # Periodic noise helps escape flat/saddle regions of the surrogate.
        if stagnation > 250 and (it % 40 == 0):
            amp = 0.06 * min(3.0, 1.0 + stagnation / 2000.0)
            y = y + rng.normal(0.0, amp, size=n)
            y -= float(np.mean(y))
            m *= 0.25
            v *= 0.25

        if verbose and (it % 1000 == 0 or it == 0):
            print(f"  adam step {it:5d}, beta={beta:9.1f}, fft_score={score:.10f}, best={best_score:.10f}")

        if stagnation > 8000:
            break

    return best_p, float(best_score)


def block_search(n, p0, rng, max_attempts, end_time, verbose=False):
    """
    Fast stochastic local search: log-normal blocks, mass swaps, and smooth
    global perturbations.  Exact FFT score is used for acceptance.
    """
    p0 = normalize_nonneg(p0, n)
    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_score = score_fft_array(p0)

    for attempt in range(max_attempts):
        if time.monotonic() > end_time:
            break

        cand = best_p.copy()
        r = rng.random()

        if r < 0.42:
            # contiguous log-normal block
            width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 250), max(2, n // 6)))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            stop = min(n, start + width)
            cand[start:stop] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25)))
        elif r < 0.72:
            # two-block mass transfer
            width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 200), max(2, n // 10)))
            start1 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            stop1 = min(n, start1 + width)

            start2 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            stop2 = min(n, start2 + width)
            if stop1 <= start2 or stop2 <= start1:
                a = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.30)))
                b = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.30)))
                cand[start1:stop1] *= a
                cand[start2:stop2] *= b
                # roughly conserve mass with a log ratio
                ratio = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35)))
                cand[start1:stop1] *= ratio
                cand[start2:stop2] *= 1.0 / max(ratio, 1e-12)
        else:
            # smooth global multiplicative perturbation in the frequency domain
            x = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)
            f = np.fft.rfft(x)
            freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
            freq[0] = 1.0
            f = f / (1.0 + 30.0 * (freq * freq))
            y = np.fft.irfft(f, n=n)
            std = float(np.std(y))
            if std > 1e-12:
                y = 1.0 + 0.55 * (y - float(np.mean(y))) / std
            else:
                y = np.ones(n)
            cand *= y

        cand = np.nan_to_num(cand, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(cand.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            continue
        cand = cand / s

        sc = score_fft_array(cand)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_p = cand
            best_score = float(sc)
            if verbose and attempt % 5000 == 0:
                print(f"  block attempt {attempt:6d}, score={best_score:.10f}")

    return best_p, float(best_score)


def make_power_pattern(n, sr, sl, eps=0.0):
    """
    Endpoint power pattern.  right is distance from the right edge, left from
    the left edge.  sr/slide closer to 0.5 gives the classical one-sided
    square-root tail; eps tames singular behavior.
    """
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    right = (float(n) - i) / float(n)
    left = (i + 1.0) / float(n)
    if eps > 0.0:
        right = right + float(eps)
        left = left + float(eps)
    val = np.power(np.maximum(right, 1e-12), -float(sr)) * np.power(np.maximum(left, 1e-12), -float(sl))
    val = np.maximum(val, 1e-300)
    return normalize_nonneg(val, n)


def make_sqrt_tame_pattern(n, ar, al):
    """Smooth tail pattern 1 / (ar + sqrt(right)) * 1 / (al + sqrt(left))."""
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    right = (float(n) - i) / float(n)
    left = (i + 1.0) / float(n)
    denom = 1.0
    if ar > 0.0:
        denom = denom * (float(ar) + np.sqrt(np.maximum(right, 1e-12)))
    if al > 0.0:
        denom = denom * (float(al) + np.sqrt(np.maximum(left, 1e-12)))
    val = 1.0 / np.maximum(denom, 1e-12)
    return normalize_nonneg(val, n)


def make_fourier_pattern(n, rng, kmodes=9, amp_scale=0.55):
    """Random low-frequency positive density."""
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = 2.0 * math.pi * i / float(n)
    val = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, kmodes + 1):
        a = rng.normal(0.0, amp_scale / (k ** 0.55))
        b = rng.normal(0.0, amp_scale / (k ** 0.55))
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        val += a * np.cos(k * theta + phase) + b * np.sin(k * theta + phase)
    val = np.maximum(val, 1e-3)
    return normalize_nonneg(val, n)


def make_smooth_random_pattern(n, rng, strength=1.0):
    """White noise low-pass filtered into a positive multiplicative perturbation of uniform."""
    x = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)
    f = np.fft.rfft(x)
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    freq[0] = 1.0
    f = f / (1.0 + 45.0 * (freq * freq))
    y = np.fft.irfft(f, n=n)
    std = float(np.std(y))
    if std > 1e-12:
        y = 1.0 + strength * (y - float(np.mean(y))) / std
    else:
        y = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.maximum(y, 1e-3)
    return normalize_nonneg(y, n)


def make_rational_pattern(n, rng):
    """Random positive rational-like density."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = rng.uniform(0.4, 2.0)
    b = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
    c = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
    d = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
    denom = a + b * x + c * (x * x) + d * (x ** 3)
    denom = np.maximum(denom, 0.05)
    val = 1.0 / denom
    return normalize_nonneg(val, n)


def make_polynomial_pattern(n, rng):
    """Random cubic polynomial clipped to positive."""
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = rng.uniform(0.2, 1.5)
    b = rng.normal(0.0, 1.2)
    c = rng.normal(0.0, 1.2)
    d = rng.normal(0.0, 1.2)
    val = a + b * x + c * (x * x) + d * (x ** 3)
    val = np.maximum(val, 1e-3)
    return normalize_nonneg(val, n)


def append_pattern(lst, p, n=None):
    if n is None:
        n = len(p)
    try:
        p = normalize_nonneg(p, n)
    except Exception:
        return
    if np.all(np.isfinite(p)) and float(p.sum()) > 0.0:
        lst.append(p)


def generate_initial_patterns(n, rng, parent_arr=None, max_patterns=80):
    """Generate diverse initial probability patterns of length n."""
    out = []
    if n <= 0:
        return out

    append_pattern(out, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)
    if parent_arr is not None:
        append_pattern(out, resample_pattern(parent_arr, n), n)

    # Power tails.
    for sr in (0.0, 0.25, 0.40, 0.45, 0.50, 0.53, 0.56):
        for eps in (0.0, 1e-4, 1e-3, 1e-2, 0.05, 0.20):
            append_pattern(out, make_power_pattern(n, sr, 0.0, eps), n)
    for sl in (0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.40):
        for eps in (0.0, 1e-3, 0.05):
            append_pattern(out, make_power_pattern(n, 0.40, sl, eps), n)
    for _ in range(14):
        sr = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.58))
        sl = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.35))
        eps = float(rng.choice((0.0, 1e-4, 1e-3, 1e-2, 0.05, 0.20)))
        append_pattern(out, make_power_pattern(n, sr, sl, eps), n)

    # Smoothly tamed tails.
    for ar in (0.0, 0.15, 0.35, 0.7, 1.2, 2.0):
        for al in (0.0, 0.15, 0.5, 1.0):
            append_pattern(out, make_sqrt_tame_pattern(n, ar, al), n)

    # Power tail times a linear modulation, often useful for balancing sides.
    for sr in (0.40, 0.50, 0.52):
        for lam in (-0.6, -0.3, 0.0, 0.3, 0.6):
            x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
            base = make_power_pattern(n, sr, 0.0, 0.0)
            val = base * (1.0 + lam * x)
            append_pattern(out, val, n)
            val2 = base * (1.0 + lam * (1.0 - x))
            append_pattern(out, val2, n)

    # Random Fourier / smooth random.
    for _ in range(10):
        append_pattern(out, make_fourier_pattern(n, rng), n)
    for strength in (0.5, 0.8, 1.2, 1.8):
        for _ in range(3):
            append_pattern(out, make_smooth_random_pattern(n, rng, strength), n)

    # Simple rational / polynomial shapes.
    for _ in range(8):
        append_pattern(out, make_rational_pattern(n, rng), n)
    for _ in range(8):
        append_pattern(out, make_polynomial_pattern(n, rng), n)

    # Cosine/sine one-modulation family.
    for amp in (0.25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0):
        for phase in (0.0, math.pi / 2, math.pi, 1.5 * math.pi):
            x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
            val = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * x + phase)
            append_pattern(out, val, n)
            val = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * x + phase)
            append_pattern(out, val, n)

    # Clip list size, keeping diversity by cap.
    if len(out) > max_patterns:
        # Deterministically keep a mix: first parent/uniform + evenly spaced rest.
        step = max(1, len(out) // max_patterns)
        out = out[0:2] + out[2::step]
        if len(out) > max_patterns:
            out = out[:max_patterns]

    return out


def add_keyed(cache, p, score, max_keep=16):
    """Maintain a list of at most max_keep (score,p) items by FFT score."""
    if not math.isfinite(score):
        return
    p = normalize_nonneg(p, len(p))
    if len(cache) < max_keep:
        cache.append((float(score), p))
        return
    worst_i = 0
    worst_s = float(cache[0][0])
    for i in range(1, len(cache)):
        if float(cache[i][0]) > worst_s:
            worst_s = float(cache[i][0])
            worst_i = i
    if float(score) < worst_s:
        cache[worst_i] = (float(score), p)


def safe_output_sequence(p):
    """Convert a numpy pattern to a valid Python list for evaluate_sequence."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if p.size <= 0:
        return [0.0]
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    mx = float(p.max()) if p.size else 0.0
    if mx > 1000.0:
        p = p * (1000.0 / mx)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones_like(p) / float(p.size)
    return [float(x) for x in p]


def exact_score(seq_list):
    """Use the official evaluator if available; fall back to FFT otherwise."""
    try:
        s = evaluate_sequence(seq_list)
        s = float(s)
        return s
    except Exception:
        try:
            return float(score_fft_array(np.asarray(seq_list, dtype=np.float64)))
        except Exception:
            return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a low-scoring nonnegative step sequence.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 20.0)

    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        n = int(parent.size)
    else:
        n = 3809
    n = max(1, min(int(n), 200000))

    if parent is not None:
        parent = normalize_nonneg(parent, n)
    else:
        parent = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    print(f"[propose] target length n={n}, budget={float(budget_s):.1f}s, parent_fft_score={score_fft_array(parent):.10f}")

    # ---------------- Phase 0: low-resolution warm start ----------------
    low_n = n
    if n > 1024:
        low_n = 1024

    low_best = []
    if low_n < n:
        low_inits = generate_initial_patterns(low_n, rng, parent_arr=parent, max_patterns=36)
        low_init_best = []
        for p in low_inits:
            s = score_fft_array(p)
            add_keyed(low_init_best, p, s, max_keep=12)

        low_deadline = min(deadline - 1200.0, start_time + 180.0)
        for idx, (s0, p0) in enumerate(low_init_best):
            if time.monotonic() > low_deadline:
                break
            p_best, s_best = adam_refine(
                low_n,
                p0,
                beta_start=2.0 * low_n,
                beta_end=30.0 * low_n,
                max_steps=3500,
                lr_start=0.18,
                lr_end=0.03,
                rng=rng,
                end_time=low_deadline,
                verbose=False,
            )
            add_keyed(low_best, p_best, s_best, max_keep=8)
            if s_best < 1.70 and idx % 2 == 0:
                print(f"[low-start] init {idx} fft_score={s_best:.10f}")

        if low_best:
            print(f"[low-start] best low fft_score={low_best[0][0]:.10f}")

    # ---------------- Phase 1: full-length initial patterns ----------------
    full_inits = generate_initial_patterns(n, rng, parent_arr=parent, max_patterns=90)

    # Include resampled low-res competitors.
    if low_n < n:
        for _, pl in low_best[:8]:
            append_pattern(full_inits, resample_pattern(pl, n), n)

    full_best = []
    for p in full_inits:
        s = score_fft_array(p)
        add_keyed(full_best, p, s, max_keep=18)

    # Deterministically sort a copy for display without lambda.
    if full_best:
        disp = [x[0] for x in full_best]
        order = np.argsort(disp)
        full_sorted = [full_best[i] for i in order]
        print(f"[init] top5 fft scores: {['%.10f' % x for x in full_sorted[:5]]}")

    # ---------------- Phase 2: full-length gradient refinement ----------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 500.0:
        # Use top several full candidates.
        if full_best:
            disp = [x[0] for x in full_best]
            order = np.argsort(disp)
            candidates = [full_best[i][1] for i in order[:10]]
        else:
            candidates = [parent]

        n_cand = len(candidates)
        reserve_block = 350.0
        for cidx, p0 in enumerate(candidates):
            remaining = max(1.0, deadline - reserve_block - time.monotonic())
            slice_s = max(15.0, min(520.0, remaining / float(max(1, n_cand - cidx))))
            end_t = time.monotonic() + slice_s
            if end_t > deadline - reserve_block:
                end_t = deadline - reserve_block
            if time.monotonic() >= end_t:
                break

            p_best, s_best = adam_refine(
                n,
                p0,
                beta_start=2.0 * n,
                beta_end=32.0 * n,
                max_steps=50000,
                lr_start=0.13,
                lr_end=0.02,
                rng=rng,
                end_time=end_t,
                verbose=False,
            )
            add_keyed(full_best, p_best, s_best, max_keep=18)
            print(f"[adam] full candidate {cidx:2d}, fft_score_before={score_fft_array(p0):.10f}, after={s_best:.10f}, time={time.monotonic()-start_time-0:.1f}s")

    # ---------------- Phase 3: stochastic block/local polish ----------------
    if full_best:
        disp = [x[0] for x in full_best]
        order = np.argsort(disp)
        polish_candidates = [full_best[i][1] for i in order[:6]]
    else:
        polish_candidates = [parent]

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
        for pidx, p0 in enumerate(polish_candidates):
            slice_end = deadline - 80.0
            p_best, s_best = block_search(
                n,
                p0,
                rng,
                max_attempts=200000,
                end_time=slice_end,
                verbose=False,
            )
            add_keyed(full_best, p_best, s_best, max_keep=20)
            print(f"[block] polish candidate {pidx:2d}, fft_score={score_fft_array(p0):.10f}, after={s_best:.10f}")

    # ---------------- Phase 4: exact evaluation of short list ----------------
    final_pool = []
    if full_best:
        for s, p in full_best:
            final_pool.append(p)
    final_pool.append(parent)

    # Final polish of very short list with a few exact-guided random tries.
    if final_pool and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        best_p = final_pool[0]
        best_fft = score_fft_array(best_p)
        for p in final_pool[1:]:
            s = score_fft_array(p)
            if s < best_fft:
                best_fft = s
                best_p = p
        tries = 200
        end_exact_polish = deadline - 30.0
        for _ in range(tries):
            if time.monotonic() > end_exact_polish:
                break
            cand = best_p.copy()
            width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 150), max(2, n // 4)))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            stop = min(n, start + width)
            cand[start:stop] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12)))
            s = float(np.sum(cand))
            if s > 0.0 and math.isfinite(s):
                cand = cand / s
                sc = score_fft_array(cand)
                if sc < best_fft:
                    best_fft = sc
                    best_p = cand
        final_pool.append(best_p)

    best_final = None
    best_exact = float("inf")

    # Evaluate parent first, then all refined candidates.
    candidate_sets = [parent]
    for p in reversed(final_pool[:20]):
        candidate_sets.append(p)

    for p in candidate_sets:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10.0:
            break
        seq = safe_output_sequence(p)
        sc = exact_score(seq)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = float(sc)
            best_final = seq

    if best_final is None:
        best_final = safe_output_sequence(parent)
        best_exact = exact_score(best_final)

    print(f"[done] best exact score={best_exact:.10f}, length={len(best_final)}, elapsed={time.monotonic()-start_time:.1f}s")
    return best_final
```
